# Changelog

All notable changes to rdom will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Upgrading from 0.5

See [`UPGRADING-0.6.md`](UPGRADING-0.6.md): the fifteen changes most 0.5 apps hit, each with its fix; an index from what you see to its cause; every change that compiles unchanged but renders or reads differently, by area, each with its stable `sc-…` anchor; the compile breaks with the `migration_hints.rs` tests that compile their new forms; and the table of renamed or reshaped API items. Each Breaking bullet below ends with its migration.

### Breaking — `rdom-core`

- **`SimpleSelector::Attribute` gains a `case: AttrCase` field**: the attribute selector's case flag (Selectors 4 §6.3). Migration: construct it with `case: AttrCase::Default` (the 0.5 behaviour: HTML §4.16.2's case-insensitive list), and match it with `{ name, op, value, .. }`. (C11-ATTR-FLAGS)

### Added — `rdom-core`

- **`selectors::MAX_SELECTOR_NESTING` (32) and `MAX_SELECTOR_SIZE` (4096)**: selector arguments (`:is` / `:not` / `:where` / `:has` / `:nth-child(… of S)`, and the lists `&` stands for) nest at most 32 deep, and `&` expansion grows a selector to at most 4096 simple selectors; past either it is invalid (a forgiving `:is()` drops the argument). (C16G-DEPTH-CAPS)
- **`selectors::pseudo_class_names()`**: the name of every pseudo-class the selector parser makes a `PseudoClass` of (`dir` for `:dir()`), read from the one table the parser now uses for its keyword pseudo-classes. (ACID-COVERAGE)
- **The column combinator `||` and `:nth-col()` / `:nth-last-col()`** (Selectors 4 §16): `Combinator::Column`, `SimpleSelector::NthColumn(NthColumnSelector)`, matched from HTML's table model (`<col>` / `<colgroup>` spans, `colspan` / `rowspan`), formed once per table per pass (`CacheWork::table_models`). (C13-COLUMN)
- **The table grid** (HTML §4.9.12.1, CSS Tables 3 §3.3): `rdom_core::table::assign_slots` places each cell of a table's row groups in its slots — `colspan` / `rowspan`, rowspans ending at their row group, `rowspan="0"` to its end — and `CellSpan::from_attributes` / `column_span` read the HTML attributes — `cell_span_of` / `column_span_of` off an element, HTML's table elements ASCII case-insensitively; a `CellSpan`'s spans (`columns()` / `rows()`) are always within HTML's ranges. Shared by rdom-tui's tables and the column combinator. (C13-TFC, C13G-COLUMN-MATCH)
- **`TopLayerKind::Picker`**: an open drop-down `<select>`'s picker in the top layer — neither `:modal` nor `:popover-open`. (C12-SELECT-TOP-LAYER)
- **`AnimationDetail`** (CSS Animations 1 §5.1): the `animationstart` / `-iteration` / `-end` / `-cancel` payload — `animation_name`, `elapsed`, `pseudo_element` — as `EventDetail::Animation`, read through `as_animation`. (C12-KEYFRAMES)
- **Pending top-layer removal** (CSS Position 4 §3.3): `Dom::request_remove_from_top_layer` keeps an element in the top layer, in its place, but no longer modal or a showing popover (`top_layer_kind` is `None`), until the backend removes it; `is_pending_top_layer_removal`, `pending_top_layer_removals`. (C12-BEHAVIOR)
- `Dom::is_default_with(id, &mut SelectorCaches)`: `:default` (HTML §4.16.3) with a pass's caches — each form's default button found once — for a backend that asks it of every control without a parsed selector. (C11G-MINOR)
- `CacheWork::chain_steps`: the candidates a combinator step of a complex selector tried — the matcher's backtracking work, by count. (C11G-HAS-COST)
- **`:has()`** (Selectors 4 §4.5): `SimpleSelector::Has(Vec<RelativeSelector>)` — relative selectors with a leading `>`, `+`, `~` or none, unforgiving, invalid inside another `:has()`, with the specificity of the most specific argument; matched against the anchor's descendants or later siblings, the rest of each selector read from there. `SelectorCaches` keeps the answers per pass (`:has(.x)` over nested anchors is linear), counts the elements searched (`CacheWork::has_nodes`) and lists the anchors it evaluated (`SelectorCaches::has_anchors()`) for a backend's invalidation. (C11-HAS)
- **`:link`, `:any-link`, `:visited`, `:lang()` and `:dir()`** (Selectors 4 §8.1–§8.2, §7.1–§7.2): `:any-link` / `:link` match `a` / `area` with `href` (HTML §4.16.3); `:visited` parses and never matches (no history); `:lang()` takes identifier or string ranges matched by RFC 4647 extended filtering against `Dom::language(id)` (HTML §3.2.6.2: the inherited `xml:lang` / `lang`, `Some("")` when unknown); `:dir(ltr | rtl)` matches `Dom::directionality(id)` (HTML §3.2.6.4: `dir`, `auto` and `<bdi>` by the first strong character, `<input type=tel>`, inheritance — the new `Directionality`), not the CSS `direction` property. (C11-LINK-LANG)
- **`:read-only` and `:read-write`** (Selectors 4 §14.3.1): HTML §4.16.3's mutability — a mutable `<input>` that `readonly` applies to (the text, date / time and number states), a mutable `<textarea>`, and any other element that is an editing host or editable (`contenteditable`, inherited, `false` stopping it) are `:read-write`; every other element is `:read-only`. `Dom::is_read_write(id)` and `Dom::is_editable_or_editing_host(id)` (HTML §6.8.1) answer it. (C11-FORM-STATES)
- **`:indeterminate` on checkboxes and radio groups** (Selectors 4 §14.4.3, HTML §4.16.3): besides a `<progress>` without `value`, it matches a checkbox with its indeterminate flag (reflected into the `indeterminate` attribute, as `checked` is) and a radio whose radio button group has no checked member. `Dom::is_indeterminate(id)`; a pass walks each radio group once (`CacheWork::radio_group_walks`). (C11-FORM-STATES)
- **`:default`, `:in-range` and `:out-of-range`** (Selectors 4 §14.4.2, §14.3.3–§14.3.4, HTML §4.16.3): `:default` matches a form's default button (its first submit button in tree order, `form=` owners included), checkboxes / radios checked by default and options selected by default; `:in-range` / `:out-of-range` a candidate for constraint validation with range limitations whose value is / is not within them. The defaults and range states are the backend's: the new `ControlState` questions and `Dom::set_control_state_hook` (without a hook the defaults are the `checked` / `selected` attributes and nothing has range limitations); `Dom::control_state`, `Dom::is_default`, `Dom::range_state`. A pass finds each form's default button once (`CacheWork::default_button_walks`). (C11-FORM-STATES)
- **`:user-valid` and `:user-invalid`** (Selectors 4 §14.4.4–§14.4.5, HTML §4.16.3): an `<input>`, `<textarea>` or `<select>` whose user validity is set and that is valid / invalid — `Dom::user_validity_state(id)`, the flag asked as `ControlState::UserValidity` (never set without a backend). (C11-FORM-STATES)
- **The top layer and `:modal`** (CSS Position 4, HTML §4.11.4, Selectors 4 §11): `Dom::top_layer()` — the document's ordered top layer — with `add_to_top_layer(id, TopLayerKind)`, `remove_from_top_layer(id)`, `top_layer_kind(id)`, `is_in_top_layer(id)`; each change fires `InteractionChanged { kind: InteractionKind::TopLayer }` (new variant) naming the element, and an element removed from the document leaves the top layer. `:modal` matches an element there as `TopLayerKind::ModalDialog`. (C11-MODAL-POPOVER)
- **`:popover-open`, `DomError::NotSupported` and `ToggleDetail::source`**: `:popover-open` matches an element in the top layer as `TopLayerKind::Popover` (Selectors 4 §11, HTML §4.16.3); `DomError::NotSupported` is the web's `NotSupportedError` (`showPopover()` on an element without `popover`); `ToggleDetail` carries `ToggleEvent.source` (`source`, `with_source`), `None` from `ToggleDetail::new`. (C11-MODAL-POPOVER)
- **`:nth-child()` and its family** (Selectors 4 §13.3–§13.4): `:nth-child(An+B [of S])`, `:nth-last-child()`, `:nth-of-type()`, `:nth-last-of-type()` (the new `SimpleSelector::Nth(NthSelector)`, with `NthKind` and `NthSelector::matches_index`), and `:first-of-type` / `:last-of-type` / `:only-of-type` (new `PseudoClass` variants), over the whole An+B microsyntax of CSS Syntax 3 §6.2 (`odd`, `even`, `-n+3`, `2n + 1`, …). Matching is linear in a sibling list's length: `Dom::matches_list_with(id, list, scope, &mut SelectorCaches)` shares a pass's nth-index cache across its elements (the query APIs use one per call), dropped by any mutation; `SelectorCaches::work()` (`CacheWork`) counts what it did. (C11-NTH)
- **Attribute selector case flags** (Selectors 4 §6.3): `[data-k="x" i]` compares the value ASCII case-insensitively, `[type="a" s]` exactly — overriding HTML §4.16.2's case-insensitive attributes either way — for every value operator; the flag is ASCII case-insensitive (`AttrCase`). (C11-ATTR-FLAGS)
- **The Custom Highlight API data model** (CSS Custom Highlight API 1 §3–§4): `Highlight` (a set of `Range`s with a `priority` and a `HighlightType`), the document's `HighlightRegistry` (`CSS.highlights`: `set` / `get` / `has` / `delete` / `clear` / `iter`, in registration order) through `Dom::highlights` / `highlights_mut`, which fires the new `Mutation::HighlightsChanged`. A registered range is live (DOM §5.3): `edit_text` / `set_data` ("replace data"), insertions and removals move its boundary points as a browser's `Range`. (C10-HIGHLIGHT)
- **Selectors decode CSS escapes** (CSS Syntax 3 §4.3.7) in names and attribute values: `.\31 0` matches class `10`. The decoder is the new public module `rdom_core::css_syntax` (`consume_escape`, `consume_ident`, `consume_string`, `would_start_ident`, …), shared with rdom-style's tokenizer. (C1-ESCAPES)
- **Nested rule selectors** (CSS Nesting 1 §2): `selectors::parse_nested(text, &parent)` — `&` anywhere, a leading combinator, an implicit `& ` prefix; `&` is the new `SimpleSelector::Is(list)` (`:is()` matching and specificity). `selectors` is a directory module. (C1-NESTING)
- **`:scope`** (Selectors 4 §14.3): `PseudoClass::Scope` matches the root given to the new `Dom::matches_list_in_scope(id, list, scope)`, else `:root`; `selectors::parse_scoped(text)` parses a scoped rule's selector (CSS Cascade 6 §2.5.2). The query APIs set it since C11-SCOPE. (C1-SCOPE)
- `css_syntax::serialize_string` / `serialize_identifier` (CSSOM §2.1): the inverses of `consume_string` / `consume_ident`. (C1G-VAR-TOKENS)
- **`:is()`** (Selectors 4 §4.2) parses into `SimpleSelector::Is`, with the specificity of its most specific argument; the list is forgiving (an unparsable argument is dropped, an empty `:is()` matches nothing). (C1G-IS-PARSE)
- **Document data** (`Dom::document_data` / `document_data_mut` / `set_document_data` / `remove_document_data`): per-document backend state, one value per Rust type; rdom-tui keeps the viewport there. (C2G-VIEWPORT-DOC)
- **`HighlightRegistry::generation()`**: a number that moves whenever a registered highlight or one of its live ranges may have changed, so a renderer can index the ranges once and rebuild the index only when it moves. (C10G-HIGHLIGHT-COST)
- **Search highlighting from Rust** (CSS Custom Highlight API 1): `Dom::descendants(root)` walks a subtree in tree order (DOM §4.2, text nodes included; the `Descendants` iterator), `Dom::range_between(a, b)` builds a `Range` from two boundary points in either order, checked as DOM §5.5 checks `setStart` / `setEnd` (an offset past `Dom::node_length`, or inside a UTF-8 character, is `InvalidOffset`), and `Dom::highlights_mut()` returns a `HighlightsMut` guard that fires `Mutation::HighlightsChanged` once, after the change, only when a highlight was set or removed or a registered one changed. `Highlight`'s members are methods named for the web's (`priority()`, `kind()` for `type`, `with_kind`); `size` is `len()` on `Highlight` and `HighlightRegistry` alike. The rdom-tui README shows a search end to end. (C10G-HIGHLIGHT-API)
- **Inertness** (HTML §6.3): `Dom::is_inert(id)` — a node outside the modal dialog the document is blocked by (`Dom::blocking_modal()`, the topmost modal dialog in the top layer), or in an `inert` subtree that no modal dialog escapes; one ancestor walk, the answer rdom-tui's focus, Tab, hit test and selection share. (C11G-MODAL-INERT)
- `EventDetail::ContentVisibilityAutoStateChange { skipped }` and `as_content_visibility_auto_state_change()`: the payload of `contentvisibilityautostatechange` (CSS Containment 2 §4, the web's `ContentVisibilityAutoStateChangeEvent`; C14G-API-NAMING). (C14-CONTAIN)

### Changed — `rdom-core`

- **`EventDetail::ContentVisibilityAutoStateChange`** (was `ContentVisibilityAutoState`, new since 0.5) takes the web event's name, and its accessor follows the `as_<variant>` pattern: `as_content_visibility_auto_state_change()`. (C14G-API-NAMING)
- **`ControlStateHook` returns `Option<bool>`**: `None` leaves a question to the substrate's default (the content attribute for `DefaultChecked` / `DefaultSelected`, else `false`), so a hook written before a `ControlState` question was added does not answer it `false`. Migration: `Some(answer)`, and `_ => None`. (C11G-API)
- **`SelectorCaches` documents that it is one pass's**: hook-backed state (user validity, default checkedness, validity) moves no mutation epoch, so caches kept across frames go stale for it. (C11G-API)
- **`Directionality`, `AttrCase` and `NthKind` are closed enums** (no `#[non_exhaustive]`): their specs fix them — `ltr` / `rtl` (HTML §3.2.6.4), no flag / `i` / `s` (Selectors 4 §6.3), the four child-indexed forms (§13.3–§13.4) — so a consumer matches them without a `_` arm. All three are new since 0.5. (C11G-DESIGN-TYPES)
- **The root fragment matches selectors as the root element** (Selectors 4 §14.1): a fragment that is the tree's root matches as an element with no name, attributes or classes — `:root`, `*`, `:root > div`, `:root:has(…)`, the user-action pseudo-classes — and no type, id, class or attribute selector; a detached fragment still matches only as a scoping root. (C14G-ROOT-ELEMENT)

### Fixed — `rdom-core`

- **A Text or Comment node takes no child** (DOM §4.2.3 pre-insertion validity, step 1): `append_child`, `insert_before` and the `ParentNode` helpers on one return `HierarchyRequest` (a Text node gained the child). (C14G-CORE-GAPS)
- **The column model stops where the table grid does** (Selectors 4 §16): `rdom_core::table::MAX_COLUMNS` (65 535) caps both, so `td:nth-col(70000)` no longer matches a cell layout gives no box; `assign_slots` skips covered columns in runs, not one by one (6.5·10⁸ steps for 100 KB of hostile spans). (C14G-CORE-GAPS)
- **The `ParentNode` / `ChildNode` helpers follow DOM §4.2.6** ("converting nodes into a node", viable siblings): `NodeMut::after([fragment, x])` failed after a partial insert, `replace_with([self])` removed the node, `replace_children` cleared before validating; each now checks every node first and inserts before the viable sibling. (C13G-DOM-CONVENIENCE)
- **`||` tries every column a cell spans, and a bare `<col>` is a column** (Selectors 4 §16.1, §3.1; HTML §13.2.6.4.9): a cell spanning two `<colgroup>`s missed `.hl col || td` when the first group's column failed it; a `<col>` child of the `<table>` before its rows (an HTML parser's implied `<colgroup>`) now counts, as in layout. (C13G-COLUMN-MATCH)
- **Moving a node records its removal** (DOM §4.2.3 "insert" → "adopt" → "remove", "replace"): `append_child` / `insert_before` of a node with a parent fire the old parent's `ChildListChanged` removal first; a fragment insertion fires one record emptying the fragment; `replace_child` names the replaced child, and replacing a child with its next sibling no longer corrupts the list. (C12G-MOVE-RECORD)
- **Removing a node under a large highlight costs a few hops per boundary point** (DOM §4.2.3 "remove" steps 4–7): each boundary's "inside the removed node?" walked to the root — 1000 search hits 60 levels deep cost 250 000 hops per removed log line; the answers now share their ancestors. (C11G-MINOR)
- **`:has()` costs what TECH_DEBT `HAS-COST-1` says** (Selectors 4 §4.5): a descendant / child argument (`:has(p div)`) is answered once per element and compound per pass — nested anchors were ~N³ — sibling-argument climbs stop at the anchor, and answers are kept per scoping root (`:scope` in `:has()` / `of S` read the wrong one). (C11G-HAS-COST)
- **Complex selectors match when any assignment of elements does** (Selectors 4 §3.1): the matcher took the nearest ancestor (or earlier sibling) matching each compound and gave up when the rest failed, so `div > p span` missed a `span` whose nearest `p` was not the `div`'s child; it now backtracks to farther candidates, bounded as Servo's matcher is. And `+` / `~` relate *element* siblings (§14.3 / §14.4): `h1 + p` matched nothing across a whitespace text node or a comment. (C11-COMBINATORS)
- **The query methods scope `:scope` to their node** (DOM §4.2.6, Selectors 4 §8.4): `query_selector_in(root, …)` / `query_selector_all_in` match with `root` as the scoping root (`:scope > p` is `root`'s own `p` children), `Dom::query_selector` / `query_selector_all` with the document, `matches(id, …)` and `closest(id, …)` with `id`; `:scope` was `:root` there. (C11-SCOPE)
- **Pseudo-class names are ASCII case-insensitive** (Selectors 4 §3.1): `a:HOVER` is `a:hover`. (C1-CASE)
- **`:first-child` / `:last-child` / `:only-child` match an element without a parent** (Selectors 4 §13.3, Level 4 drops the parent requirement), as `:nth-child(1)` does. (C11-NTH)
- **Live highlight ranges follow a `DocumentFragment` insertion** (DOM §4.2.3 "insert" step 4): a range inside the fragment's children moves to `(fragment, 0)` as they leave it; and appending, or inserting or removing where no boundary is, walks no siblings while a highlight is registered. (C10G-HIGHLIGHT-COST)

### Breaking — `rdom-style`

- **The transition, animation, timeline and grid fields move into two groups**: `TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle`'s `transition_*`, `animation_*`, timeline and range fields are in `motion` (`MotionDeclarations` / `MotionStyle`), the `grid_*` fields in `grid` (`GridDeclarations` / `GridStyle`), both `Shared` — `ComputedStyle` 2 536 → 1 696 bytes, `TuiStyle` 2 984 → 2 152. Migration: `c.transition_duration` → `c.motion.transition_duration`; builders unchanged. (C15G-STYLE-SIZE)
- **The rare style groups are `Shared`**: `ComputedStyle::{effects, multicol, anchor, ui}` and `TuiStyle::{effects, masks, multicol, anchor, ui}` hold a `Shared<…>` (copy on write), so a style that sets none shares one default — `ComputedStyle` 3 072 → 2 536 bytes, `TuiStyle` 3 912 → 2 984. Migration: fields read and write as before; `*c.effects` names the group, `.into()` builds one. (C15G-STYLE-SIZE)
- **`CalcExpr` gains `Anchor`; `ComputedStyle` / `TuiStyle` gain `anchor`** (CSS Anchor Positioning 1: `anchor()` / `anchor-size()` as math leaves; `AnchorStyle` / `AnchorDeclarations`). Migration: a `CalcExpr` walker adds the arm (`f.fallback()`, or `substitute_anchors`); a pattern adds `anchor: _`. (C15-ANCHOR)
- **`ComputedStyle` / `TuiStyle` gain `multicol` and `fragmentation`** (`MulticolStyle` / `FragmentationStyle`, CSS Multi-column 1, Fragmentation 3). Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a pattern adds `multicol: _, fragmentation: _`. (C15-COLUMNS)
- **`ComputedStyle` / `TuiStyle` gain `effects`** (`EffectsStyle` / `EffectsDeclarations`): `translate`, `rotate`, `scale`, `transform`, `transform-origin` and `transform-box`, the group the filter and compositing properties join. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a pattern adds `effects: _`. (C15-TRANSLATE)
- **`Display` gains `TablePart(TablePart)` and `Flow` gains `Table`** (CSS 2.1 §17.2, CSS Display 3 §2.2 / §2.4): `display: table` / `inline-table` and the layout-internal table keywords; `ComputedStyle` / `TuiStyle` gain `table` (`TableStyle` / `TableDeclarations`: `table-layout`, `caption-side`). Migration: a `match` adds the arms; a pattern adds `table: _`. (C13-TFC)
- **The tokenizer's cursor is `parse::SourceCursor`** (module `parse::source_cursor`): `Cursor` is the `cursor` property's value (`layout::Cursor`), and two public `Cursor`s collided under a glob import. Migration: `rdom_style::parse::Cursor` → `parse::SourceCursor`, same methods. (C12G-API-HYGIENE)
- **`TimingFunction` is not `Copy`, gains `LinearStops`** (`linear()`, CSS Easing 2) **and is `#[non_exhaustive]`** (C12G-API-HYGIENE); **`transition-delay` is signed**: `TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle::transition_delay` are `Vec<i32>`, `TransitionRule::delay_ms` is `i32`, `parse_time_list` reads signed delays (`parse_duration_list` the durations). Migration: `.clone()` an easing; a match adds a `_` arm; `i32` delays. (C12-TIMING, C12G-API-HYGIENE)
- **`Size` gains `CalcSize`** (`calc-size()`, CSS Values 5 §10), the value an `auto` ↔ length transition runs through. Migration: add a `Size::CalcSize(c)` arm — size it as `c.basis_size()`, or `c.resolve(basis, percent_basis)` once the basis is known. (C12-ANIMATABLE)
- **`MinSize`, `MaxSize` and `FlexBasis` gain `CalcSize`**, and `CalcSizeBasis` gains `Content` (`flex-basis: calc-size(content, …)`). Migration: add a `CalcSize(c)` arm — its keyword is `c.basis_min_size()` / `basis_max_size()` / `basis_flex_basis()`, its size `c.resolve(basis, percent_basis)`. (C12-ANIMATABLE)
- **`transition-property` names any property**: `TransitionProperty::Named` holds a `PropertyName` — a known property's canonical name, built only by `PropertyName::new` (`None` for `colour`) — and any other name is `Other(name)`; `AnimatableProperty` and `parse_animatable_property` are gone. Migration: `TransitionProperty::named("color")`; match `Named(n)`, read `n.as_str()`. (C12-ANIMATABLE, C12G-API-HYGIENE)
- **`Length::Calc` holds an `Arc<CalcExpr>`** (was a `Box`): an inherited `calc()` `text-indent` is shared by its descendants' computed styles, not copied per element, and every `Length` clones without allocating. Migration: `Length::Calc(Box::new(e))` → `Length::calc(e)` (or `Length::Calc(Arc::new(e))`); a match on `Length::Calc(e)` reads `e` as before. (C9-CARRY-INDENT)
- **`PseudoElementTarget` is no longer `Copy`**: its new `Highlight(Arc<str>)` variant names a `::highlight()`'s highlight. Migration: `rule.pseudo.clone()` where a copy was taken; a `match` adds `Highlight(_)` (the enum is `#[non_exhaustive]`). (C10-HIGHLIGHT)
- **`CounterOp` is `#[non_exhaustive]`** and gains `reversed` and `value_given` (`counter-reset: reversed(c)`, CSS Lists 3 §4.2); `parse_counter_ops` takes a third argument, whether `reversed()` is allowed. Migration: `CounterOp { name, value }` → `CounterOp::new(name, value)`; `CounterOp::reversed(name, Some(n))` for a reversed reset. (C10-COUNTERS)
- **`ComputedStyle` gains `counter_set`**. Migration: a destructuring pattern adds `counter_set: _`. (C10-COUNTERS)
- **`ComputedStyle` gains `list_style_type`, `list_style_position`, `list_style_image` and `marker_side`**; `content: normal` is `Content::Normal` (was `Content::None`; on `::before` / `::after` it still generates nothing). Migration: a destructuring pattern adds the four fields; a `match` on `Content` treating `None` as "no content" adds `Normal`. (C10-LIST-ITEM)
- **`CounterStyle` is a counter style name** (CSS Counter Styles 3 §3: `<counter-style-name>`), no longer a closed `Copy` enum of five styles: `CounterStyle::Name(CounterStyleName)` (`#[non_exhaustive]`; a predefined name is `'static`, an author's shared), built with `decimal()` / `disc()` / `named(name)` / `parse(ident)` / `symbols(system, symbols)`, read with `name()` (`None` for `symbols()`) and `to_css()`; an undefined name formats as `decimal`. `counters` is a module directory (`rdom_style::counters::CounterStyle` stays). Migration: `CounterStyle::Decimal` → `CounterStyle::decimal()`, `CounterStyle::UpperRoman` → `CounterStyle::named("upper-roman")`, `as_str()` → `name()` (an `Option`); a `match` on the old variants compares `name()`. (C10-COUNTERS)
- **`Content` is `#[non_exhaustive]` and holds the full `content` grammar** (CSS Generated Content 3 §2): new `Counters { name, separator, style }` (`counters()`), `Quote(QuoteKind)` (`open-quote`, `close-quote`, `no-open-quote`, `no-close-quote`) and `WithAlt { content, alt }` (`<content-list> / <alt text>`); `ContentContext` gains the provided `counters(name)` and `quote(kind)`; `ComputedStyle` gains `content_alt` (the resolved alt text) and `content_quotes`. Migration: a `match` on `Content` adds a wildcard arm; a destructuring `ComputedStyle` pattern adds `content_alt: _, content_quotes: _`. (C10-CONTENT)
- **`ComputedStyle` gains `quotes`** (`Quotes`). Migration: a destructuring pattern adds `quotes: _`. (C10-QUOTES)
- **`font-weight` and `font-style` are font longhands** (CSS Fonts 4): `TuiStyle::bold` / `italic` (`Option<Value<bool>>`) are replaced by `TuiStyle::font` (`FontDeclarations`: `weight`, `style`, `size`, `family`, `stretch`, `variant`); `ComputedStyle::font` (`Font`) holds them, the weight computed to a number, and `ComputedStyle::modifiers`' bold and italic are derived from it. `ImportantMask::BOLD` / `ITALIC` stay, as `FONT_WEIGHT` / `FONT_STYLE`. Migration: `style.bold` → `style.font.weight` (`FontWeight::Bold`), `style.italic` → `style.font.style` (`FontStyle::Italic`); the `.bold(true)` / `.italic(true)` builders are unchanged. (C9-FONT)
- **`text-decoration` is the shorthand of four longhands** (CSS Text Decoration 4 §2): `TuiStyle::text_decoration` is a `TextDecorationDeclarations` (`line`, `style`, `color`, `thickness`), was `Option<Value<TextDecoration>>`; `ImportantMask::TEXT_DECORATION` is the union of `TEXT_DECORATION_LINE` / `_STYLE` / `_COLOR` / `_THICKNESS`; `parse::values::parse_text_decoration` returns a `TextDecorationShorthand` (was `Option<(bool, bool)>`); the computed decorations are `ComputedStyle::text_decoration` / `applied_decorations`, no longer `Modifier::UNDERLINED` / `CROSSED_OUT` bits in `ComputedStyle::modifiers`. Migration: `style.text_decoration` → `style.text_decoration.line` (`TextDecorationLine::UNDERLINE`); the `.text_decoration(TextDecoration::Underline)` builder is unchanged; read `computed.applied_decorations.underline.is_some()` for "is this text underlined". (C9-DECORATION)
- **`white-space` is the shorthand of `white-space-collapse` and `text-wrap-mode`** (CSS Text 4 §3): `TuiStyle::white_space` is replaced by `TuiStyle::text` (`TextDeclarations`, one field per CSS Text longhand), `ComputedStyle::white_space` by `ComputedStyle::text` (`TextStyle`, inherited whole) (`ImportantMask::WHITE_SPACE` stays, the union of the two longhands' bits `WHITE_SPACE_COLLAPSE` / `TEXT_WRAP_MODE`); `WhiteSpace` (moved to `layout/text.rs`, still `rdom_style::layout::WhiteSpace`) gains `PreLine` and `BreakSpaces`. Migration: `style.white_space` → `style.text.white_space_collapse` / `text_wrap_mode`; `computed.white_space` → `computed.text.white_space()` (the keyword the longhands spell, `None` for a pair no keyword names); `.white_space(WhiteSpace::Pre)` still sets both; an exhaustive `match` on `WhiteSpace` adds the two arms. (C9-WHITE-SPACE)
- **`scroll-padding-*` / `scroll-margin-*` are `Sides`**, as `margin` / `padding` are: `TuiStyle::scroll_padding` / `scroll_margin` (`Sides<Option<Value<…>>>`) and `ComputedStyle::scroll_padding` (`Sides<ScrollPadding>`) / `scroll_margin` (`Sides<i16>`) replace the four loose fields each (added after 0.5). Migration: `style.scroll_padding_top` → `style.scroll_padding.top`; the `scroll_padding_top(…)` builders are unchanged. (C8G-API-TYPES)
- **`ScrollbarGutter` gains `StableBothEdges`** (`scrollbar-gutter: stable both-edges`, CSS Overflow 3 §3.3), and `is_stable()` / `both_edges()` / `keyword()`; it moved to `layout/scrollbar.rs` (still `rdom_style::layout::ScrollbarGutter`). Migration: an exhaustive `match` adds the variant — or asks `is_stable()` where it matched `Stable`. (C8-SCROLLBAR)
- **`Overflow` has a `Clip` variant** (CSS Overflow 3 §3.1): an exhaustive `match` adds the arm. (C8-OVERFLOW-CLIP)
- **`ZIndex::Value` holds an `i32`** (was `i16`): `z-index` takes any `<integer>`, a value past `i32` clamped (CSS 2.1 §9.9.1, CSS Values 4 §5.1). Migration: a match arm binding the value gets an `i32`; `ZIndex::Value(n)` with an `i16` takes `n.into()`. (C8-Z-INDEX)
- **`ComputedStyle::min_width` / `min_height` are `MinSize`** (were `Option<MinSize>`), initial `MinSize::Auto` (CSS Sizing 3 §5.2); an explicit `min-*: auto` no longer floors a flex item's cross size. Migration: `None` → `MinSize::Auto`, `Some(m)` → `m`; `.cells(basis)` for cells. (C3G-MIN-AUTO)
- **`Value<T>` gains `Revert`** (`revert`, CSS Cascade 4 §7.3), kept as written for the cascade to resolve (`unset` is still resolved at parse time); `Value` is closed data. Migration: add a `Value::Revert` arm — in a cascade roll back to the UA origin's value, elsewhere treat it like the other keywords. (C1-REVERT)
- **`Value<T>` gains `RevertLayer`** (`revert-layer`, CSS Cascade 5 §7.4), resolved by the cascade like `Revert`. Migration: add a `Value::RevertLayer` arm next to `Value::Revert`. (C1-LAYER)
- **`var()` declarations are kept as CSS on `TuiStyle::pending`** (`Vec<PendingDeclaration>`, CSS Variables 1 §3), with a block's later declarations in order; `color: var(--x)` no longer parses to `TuiColor::Var`; `parse_var_args` is removed. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()`; resolve with `TuiStyle::substituted(&vars)`. (C1-VAR-ANY)
- **`ComputedStyle` gains `animated_vars: Option<VarMap>`**: the custom properties with running transitions applied, which `var()` reads (Properties and Values 1 §6.2). Migration: build from `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds `animated_vars: _`. (C1-PROPERTY)
- **A number followed by an identifier is one `Token::Dimension { value, integer, unit }`** (CSS Syntax 3 §4.3.3); a parser matching the `Number` / `Float` + `Ident` pair no longer sees it, and `width: 1 fr` is invalid. Migration: match `Token::Dimension { value, unit, .. }`. (C1G-VAR-TOKENS)
- **Custom property values are `CustomValue`s** (text plus tokens, tokenized once): `VarMap`, `CustomDeclaration::value`, `Stylesheet::vars()` and the substitution functions use them. Migration: see the API table; `value.as_str()` reads the text, `"text".into()` builds one. (C1G-VAR-COST)
- **Registration errors are typed**: `PropertyRegistration::new` returns `Result<_, RegisterPropertyError>`, `PropertySyntax::parse` `Result<_, PropertySyntaxError>` (`#[non_exhaustive]`, `Display` + `Error`, messages unchanged). Migration: match the variant with a `_` arm, or `.to_string()`. (C1G-TYPED-ERRORS)
- **`min-*` / `max-*` take percentages and math, `max-*` takes `none`**: `MinSize` gains `Percent` / `Calc`, `max-*` is the new `MaxSize` (was `u16`); both resolve with `cells(basis)`. Migration: see the API table; `.max_width(20)` is unchanged. (C2-PERCENT, C2G-MAX-NONE, C3G-API, C4G-REEXPORTS)
- **Flex factors are `f32`**: `Size::Flex(f32)` and `flex_shrink: f32` (`TuiStyle`, `ComputedStyle`, builders). Migration: `Size::Flex(1)` → `Size::Flex(1.0)`, `.flex_shrink(0)` → `.flex_shrink(0.0)`. (C2-NUMBER)
- **`CalcExpr` gains `Function { func, args }`, `NoBound` and `Dimension { value, unit }`** (closed data). Migration: add the arms, or resolve with `CalcExpr::resolve` / `resolve_f64` instead of walking it. (C2-MINMAX)
- **`AspectRatio` holds `f32` terms and `auto` behind accessors** (`numerator()`, `value()`, …; `AspectRatio::new(f32, f32)` rejects negative or non-finite terms; a zero term is degenerate, `auto`). Migration: see the API table. (C2-RATIO, C2G-LAYOUT-SAFETY)
- **`parse::values::parse_unsigned` is removed** (unused). Migration: `parse_calc` and resolve, or match the integer token. (C2G-CALC-SEMANTICS)
- **`parse_flex_shorthand` returns `FlexShorthand { grow, shrink, basis }`** (was `Option<Size>`); `TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `flex_basis` (`FlexBasis`, initial `Auto`) and `ImportantMask::FLEX_BASIS`. Migration: map `grow` to a `Size` yourself; a struct literal adds `flex_basis`. (C2G-FLEX-SHORTHAND)
- **`Content::Attr` and `ContentContext::attr` are removed**: `content: attr(x)` is an `attr()` substitution the cascade makes (CSS Values 5 §8.7). Migration: declare it as CSS (`property_dispatch::set("content", "attr(x)", &mut style)`, or a sheet); drop `ContentContext::attr`. (C2G-CONTENT-ATTR)
- **`calc::round_half_to_even` is removed.** Migration: `v.round_ties_even() as i32`, or `calc::to_cells(v)` for a top-level result. (C2G-CELLS-CONVERSIONS)
- **`parse_content` no longer parses `attr()`**: a value holding one returns `None`, and `property_dispatch::set` keeps it on `pending`. Migration: set `content` through `property_dispatch::set` or a sheet; outside a cascade, `TuiStyle::substituted_pending(vars, &cx.with_attrs(&lookup))`. (C2-ATTR, C2G-DOCS)
- **`Color` gains `Rgba(r, g, b, a)`** (CSS Color 4 §4.2) with `Color::rgba` (an opaque alpha is `Rgb`), `alpha()`, `is_translucent()`, `opaque()`, `TRANSPARENT`; hex `#rgba` / `#rrggbbaa` and `rgb()` keep their alpha. Migration: add a `Color::Rgba` arm, or match `c.opaque()`; build with `Color::rgba`. (C3-RGB)
- **`TuiColor` gains `CurrentColor`** (CSS Color 4 §6.4) and `resolve_tui_color` takes a `&ColorContext` (`current_color`, the scheme). Migration: add a `TuiColor::CurrentColor` arm; pass `&ColorContext::new(element_color).with_scheme(scheme)` (`new` alone is dark). (C3-CURRENTCOLOR)
- **`TuiColor` gains `Function(ColorFunction)`**: an element-dependent color function (`color-mix(in srgb, currentcolor, blue)`), kept parsed with its CSS text (`css_text()`) and computed by `compute(cx)` (or `TuiColor::resolve`). Migration: add the arm — `f.compute(&ColorContext::new(element_color))` or `f.css_text()`. (C3-MIX)
- **`TuiColor` gains `System(SystemColor)`** (CSS Color 4 §6.2), serialized as its keyword. Migration: add the arm — `s.color()` is its value, `s.definite(scheme)` a definite sRGB color. (C3-SYSTEM)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `color_scheme`** (`color-scheme`, CSS Color Adjust 1 §2; `ColorSchemeList`, initial `normal`) and `ImportantMask::COLOR_SCHEME`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds `color_scheme`. (C3-SCHEME)
- **`TuiStyle` gains the seven `background_*` longhands** (`image` … `clip`) and their `BACKGROUND_*` bits; `background` owns all eight fields and is no longer dropped when not a lone color. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()`; a destructuring pattern adds the fields. (C4-BACKGROUND)
- **`ComputedStyle` gains `background_clip: VisualBox`** (the last layer's, initial `BorderBox`). Migration: build from `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds it. (C4-BG-CLIP)
- **Border colors are per side, border widths are longhands**: `border_fg` is `border_color: Sides<…>`, `TuiStyle` gains `border_width`, `BORDER_FG` is `BORDER_*_COLOR` (+ `BORDER_*_WIDTH`); the `border_fg*` builders set all four sides. Migration: see the API table. (C4-BORDER-SHORTHAND)
- **`ComputedStyle::border` is the used border** (zero-width sides `none`, CSS Backgrounds 3 §4.3); `ComputedStyle` gains `border_style` (cascaded) and `border_width`. Migration: read `border_style` for a declared style; build from `ComputedStyle::initial()`. (C4-BORDER-WIDTH)
- **Border styles are per-side longhands, corners are radii**: `TuiStyle::border` is `border_style: Sides<…>`, `border_radius: Corners<…>` is new, `Border` loses `corner_style` / `rounded()`, `BORDER` is `BORDER_*_STYLE`; `.border(Border)` still takes a `Border`. Migration: see the API table. (C4-RADIUS)
- **`TuiStyle` gains `box_shadow`** (`Option<Value<Vec<BoxShadow>>>`; `ComputedStyle::box_shadow: Vec<BoxShadow<Color>>`, colors resolved) and `ImportantMask::BOX_SHADOW`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds `box_shadow`. (C4-SHADOW)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `border_spacing`** (inherited, `ImportantMask::BORDER_SPACING`). Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds it. (C4-SPACING)
- **`ImportantMask` is an opaque bitset**, one bit per field: `bits()` / `from_bits_truncate` are removed, and each `transition-*` longhand has its own bit (`TRANSITIONS` is their union). Migration: `contains(ImportantMask::FG)` / `is_empty()`, combine with `|` / `&` / `without`. (C4G-IMPORTANT-BITSET)
- **`border` and `border-<side>` reset the color and the width** (CSS Backgrounds 3 §4.4): `border: solid` makes the sides `currentcolor` and `medium`, so an earlier `border-color` / `border-width` no longer survives. Migration: put them after `border`, or into it (`border: thick solid red`). (C4-BORDER-SHORTHAND)
- **`border: rounded` sets `border-radius`, which a later `border` keeps** (as on the web): `.card { border: rounded } .card.flat { border: solid }` stays rounded. Migration: `border-radius: 0` squares the corners. (C4-RADIUS)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `box_sizing`** (`BoxSizing::{ContentBox, BorderBox}`, initial `ContentBox`, CSS UI 3 §3.1), `ImportantMask::BOX_SIZING` and builders. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds `box_sizing`. (C5-BOX-SIZING)
- **`Size`, `MinSize` and `MaxSize` gain `Intrinsic(IntrinsicSize)`** — `min-content`, `max-content`, `fit-content`, `fit-content(<length-percentage>)` (CSS Sizing 3 §3.1–§3.3); `cells()` is `None` for one. Migration: add an `Intrinsic(_)` arm (as `auto` where only lengths matter). (C5-INTRINSIC)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `margin_trim`** (`MarginTrim`, initial `NONE`, CSS Box 4 §3), `ImportantMask::MARGIN_TRIM`, builders and `parse_margin_trim`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds it. (C5-MARGIN-TRIM)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `contain_intrinsic_width` / `_height`** (`ContainIntrinsicSize`, initial `none`, CSS Sizing 4 §6.1) with their bits and builders. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C5-CONTAIN-SIZE)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `text_direction` and `writing_mode`** (CSS `direction` and `writing-mode`, inherited) with their bits and builders (the `direction` field stays `flex-direction`). Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C5-WRITING)
- **`Token::Number` holds an `i64`** (was `i32`): an integer keeps its value, and each `<integer>` property clamps to its own range (CSS Values 4 §5.1). Migration: widen a matched `Token::Number(n)` (`n as f64`, or `i32::try_from(n)`). (C5G-INT-CLAMP-SITE)
- **`ImportantMask::DIRECTION` is `FLEX_DIRECTION`** (the `flex-direction` bit; `TEXT_DIRECTION` is CSS `direction`). Migration: rename the constant. (C5G-API-EDGES)
- **`margin` / `padding` are four longhands each** (CSS Box 3 §3.2 / §4.2): `TuiStyle::margin` / `padding` are `Sides<Option<Value<…>>>` with a bit per side, and a side cascades alone; `ComputedStyle` keeps `Margin` / `Padding`. Migration: see the API table. (C6-MARGIN-SIDES)
- **`display` takes the full CSS Display 3 §2 syntax**: `Display` gains `Contents`, `Flow` gains `FlowRoot`, the styles gain `list_item`, and `display: inline` also writes the inner type `flow`. Migration: add `Display::Contents` / `Flow::FlowRoot` arms; build with `TuiStyle::new()`. (C6-DISPLAY-KEYWORDS)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `visibility`** (CSS Display 3 §4, inherited) with its bit and builders; `AnimatableProperty` gains `Visibility`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds it. (C6-VISIBILITY)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `order`** (`<integer>`, initial 0, CSS Flexbox §5.4), `ImportantMask::ORDER`, builders and `parse_order`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds `order`. (C6-ORDER)
- **`flex-direction` takes `row-reverse` / `column-reverse`** (CSS Flexbox §5.1): the styles gain `flex_reverse` (the property's mask is `FLEX_DIRECTION | FLEX_REVERSE`); `direction()` writes it `false`, `direction_reverse()` `true`. Migration: a destructuring pattern adds `flex_reverse`. (C6-DIRECTION-REVERSE)
- **`flex` sets `flex-grow` / `flex-shrink` / `flex-basis`, not `width`** (CSS Flexbox §7.2): the styles gain `flex_grow` (`f32`) with `FLEX_GROW` and builders; `FlexBasis` gains `Intrinsic`. Migration: read `flex_grow` where you read `Size::Flex(grow)`; add a `FlexBasis::Intrinsic(_)` arm. (C6-FLEX-LONGHANDS)
- **`gap` is two longhands** (CSS Box Alignment 3 §8): `gap` is `row_gap` / `column_gap` on both styles, and `GapValue` gains `Normal` (the initial value, 0 in flex). Migration: see the API table; test `contains(ImportantMask::ROW_GAP | ImportantMask::COLUMN_GAP)` for `GAP`. (C6-GAP)
- **`flex-direction`'s initial value is `row`** (CSS Flexbox §5.1; was `column`): `Direction::default()` is `Row`, and rdom-tui ignores `flex-direction` on block containers. Migration: add `flex-direction: column` (`.flex_column()`) to every flex container that relied on the old default. (C6-FLEX-DIRECTION-INITIAL)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `flex_wrap`** (CSS Flexbox §5.2) with `FLEX_WRAP` and builders; `flex-wrap` and `flex-flow` parse (`parse_flex_wrap` / `parse_flex_flow`). Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()`; a destructuring pattern adds `flex_wrap`. (C6-WRAP)
- **`Align` is the Box Alignment vocabulary** (CSS Box Alignment 3 §4; was four keywords, default `Start`, now `Normal`), with the new `Alignment` value; the styles gain `justify_content` with its bit and builders. Migration: match the new variants; a destructuring pattern adds `justify_content`. (C6-JUSTIFY)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `align_items` and `align_self`** (CSS Box Alignment 3 §6.3 / §6.1) with `ALIGN_ITEMS` / `ALIGN_SELF`, builders and `parse_align_items` / `parse_align_self`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C6-ALIGN)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `align_content`** (CSS Box Alignment 3 §5.1) with `ALIGN_CONTENT`, builders and `parse_align_content`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds it. (C6-ALIGN-CONTENT)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `justify_items` (initial `Alignment::LEGACY`) and `justify_self`** (CSS Box Alignment 3 §6.2 / §6.1) with bits and builders; the `place-*` shorthands parse. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C6-PLACE)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `grid_template_columns` / `grid_template_rows`** (CSS Grid 2 §7.2, `GridTemplate` of `TrackList` / `TrackSize`) with bits, builders and `parse_grid_template` / `serialize_grid_template`. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C7-GRID-CORE)
- **`Flow` gains `Grid`**: `display: grid` / `inline-grid` (and `block grid` / `inline grid`) are its outer `Block` / `Inline` pairs; `Flow::is_flex_or_grid()` is "its children are items". Migration: add a `Flow::Grid` arm; `.grid()` / `.inline_grid()` build it. (C7-GRID-CORE)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `grid_auto_columns` / `grid_auto_rows`** (CSS Grid 2 §7.6; declared `Vec<TrackSize>`, computed `Cow<'static, [TrackSize]>`) with bits, builders and parsers. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C7-GRID-AUTO)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain the grid placement fields** `grid_row_start` / `_end`, `grid_column_start` / `_end` (`GridLine`) and `grid_auto_flow` (CSS Grid 2 §8.3, §7.7), with bits, builders and parsers. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds them. (C7-GRID-PLACE)
- **`TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` gain `grid_template_areas`** (CSS Grid 2 §7.3: `GridTemplateAreas`, valid by construction — `GridTemplateAreas::new` refuses non-rectangles) with a bit, a builder and a parser. Migration: build with `TuiStyle::new()` / `ComputedStyle::initial()`; a destructuring pattern adds it. (C7-GRID-AREAS)
- **The alignment builders take `impl Into<Alignment>` and check the property's grammar; `Align` is `#[non_exhaustive]`**: an out-of-grammar value panics in a debug build and sets nothing in a release one. Migration: drop the `.into()` on an `Align` argument; add a `_` arm to a `match` on `Align`. (C6G-ALIGN-API)
- **`property_dispatch::set_from_source` takes `text: Option<&str>` and `important: bool`** (one call per parsed declaration), and **`parse::token::SpannedTokens` is a struct** (`tokens`, `positions`, `spans`). Migration: see the API table. (C6G-FRONTEND-API)
- **Every `calc()` payload is an `Arc<CalcExpr>`** (was a `Box`), as `Length`'s is: `Size`, `MinSize`, `MaxSize`, `GapValue`, `PaddingValue`, `MarginValue`, `FlexBasis`, `Spacing`, `LineHeight`, `VerticalAlign`, `PaintLength`, `TrackBreadth`. Migration: `Size::Calc(Box::new(e))` → `Size::calc(e)`; a `Calc(e)` match reads `e` as before. (C10G-INHERIT-COST)

### Added — `rdom-style`

- **`conditional::MAX_CONDITION_NESTING` (32)**: an `@media` / `@supports` / `@container` prelude nesting parentheses deeper does not parse (`not all`; an invalid rule); and `SourceCursor::block_depth` / `in_block`, the block depth a parser caps its recursion by. (C16G-DEPTH-CAPS)
- **`PseudoElementTarget::named()`**: every pseudo-element target a selector can end in, from the selector-text splitter's own tables (`::highlight()` once, unnamed). (ACID-COVERAGE)
- **`MotionStyle` / `MotionDeclarations` and `GridStyle` / `GridDeclarations`**: the transition, animation and timeline lists and the grid properties as two shared groups of `ComputedStyle` / `TuiStyle` (`motion`, `grid`); re-exported at the `rdom_tui` root. (C15G-STYLE-SIZE)
- **`Shared<T>`**: a style group behind a shared pointer, copied on write (`Deref` / `DerefMut`, `Shared::new`, `From`, `ptr_eq`, one `Default` per group type); re-exported at the `rdom_tui` root. (C15G-STYLE-SIZE)
- **The anchor positioning properties** (CSS Anchor Positioning 1 §2–§5): `anchor-name`, `anchor-scope`, `position-anchor`, `position-area`, `position-try` / `-fallbacks` / `-order`, `position-visibility`; `anchor()` / `anchor-size()` in insets, sizes and margins (`AnchorFunction`); `anchor-center`; `PositionTryRule`. (C15-ANCHOR)
- **`position-area: none` is `PositionArea::NONE`** (the initial value and `Default`; `is_none()`), so `.position_area(PositionArea::NONE)` takes the type as `.position_anchor(PositionAnchor::None)` does; `keywords()` / `tracks()` return `None` for it. `TryFallback::tactics(vec![FlipBlock])` spells a tactics-only option. (C15G-API)
- **The multi-column and fragmentation properties** (CSS Multi-column 1 §3–§7, Fragmentation 3 §3, §5.4): `columns`, `column-count` / `-width` / `-rule*` / `-span` / `-fill`, `break-before` / `-after` / `-inside`, the legacy `page-break-*`, `orphans` / `widows` (inherited), `box-decoration-break` — parsed, serialized, interpolated (`MulticolStyle`, `FragmentationStyle`, `ComputedStyle::is_multicol_container`, …). (C15-COLUMNS)
- **`clip-path` and the `mask*` properties** (CSS Masking 1 §5–§7, CSS Shapes 1 §3.1): `ClipPath`, `BasicShape` (`inset()`, `circle()`, `ellipse()`, `polygon()`, `path()`), `GeometryBox`, `ShapeRadius`, with cell-centre geometry (`BasicShape::contains`) and interpolation; the mask longhands and shorthands kept as text (`MaskDeclarations`). (C15-CLIP-PATH)
- **`mix-blend-mode`, `isolation` and `background-blend-mode`** (Compositing and Blending 1 §3.2, §3.4, §5.2; Compositing 2's `plus-darker` / `plus-lighter`): `BlendMode` with §10's separable and non-separable formulas (`BlendMode::blend`), `Isolation`; not animatable. (C15-BLEND)
- **`filter` and `backdrop-filter`** (Filter Effects 1 §5–§6, 2 §3): the color-matrix functions, `opacity()`, `drop-shadow()`, `blur()` and `url()`, parsed, serialized and interpolated (`FilterList`, `FilterFunction`) with the sRGB color math (`FilterList::filter_rgb`). (C15-FILTER)
- **The transform properties** (CSS Transforms 1 / 2): `translate`, `transform` (translate functions in cells, every other function checked and kept inert), `rotate`, `scale`, `transform-origin`, `transform-box` — parsed, serialized, interpolated (`Translate`, `TransformList`, `TransformFunction`, …); `Longhand::moves_boxes`. (C15-TRANSLATE)
- **`empty-cells: show | hide`** (CSS 2.1 §17.6.1.1, inherited): `EmptyCells`, `TableStyle::empty_cells`, the `empty_cells` builder. (C13-TABLE-PROPS)
- **Builder setters for every scroll-driven longhand**: `view_timeline_axis`, `view_timeline_inset` and `timeline_scope` join `scroll_timeline_*` / `view_timeline_name`; the CSS UI setters (`cursor`, `appearance`, `outline_*`, …) take `impl Into` of their value, so `.cursor(CursorKeyword::Pointer)`. (C12G-API-HYGIENE)
- **`resize`** (CSS UI 4 §4.2): `none | both | horizontal | vertical | block | inline`, not inherited, discrete (`Resize`, `axes()`); the UA sheet's `textarea` takes `both`. (C12-CONTROLS)
- **`field-sizing`** (CSS UI 4 §7.2): `content | fixed`, not inherited, discrete (`FieldSizing`). (C12-CONTROLS)
- **`appearance`** (CSS UI 4 §7.1) and its legacy name `-webkit-appearance`: `none | auto | base` and the compat keywords, not inherited, discrete (`Appearance`). (C12-CONTROLS)
- **`accent-color`** (CSS UI 4 §6.3): `auto | <color>`, inherited, interpolated between colors (`AccentColor` in `ComputedStyle::ui`). (C12-CONTROLS)
- **`caret-shape`, `caret-animation` and the `caret` shorthand** (CSS UI 4 §6.2): `auto | bar | block | underscore` and `auto | manual`, inherited and discrete (`CaretShape`, `CaretAnimation` in `ComputedStyle::ui`), the shorthand also setting `caret-color`; `parse_caret_color` is the one `caret-color` parser. (C12-CARET)
- **`cursor`** (CSS UI 4 §4.1): every `<cursor-predefined>` keyword after `url()` fallbacks with optional hotspots (kept and serialized; a terminal draws no image), inherited, discrete — `Cursor`, `CursorKeyword`, `CursorImage` in `ComputedStyle::ui`. (C12-CURSOR)
- **The outline properties** (CSS UI 4 §5): `outline-style` (`auto` and every line style but `hidden`), `outline-width`, `outline-color` (`auto | <color>`), `outline-offset` and the `outline` shorthand — in `TuiStyle::ui` / `ComputedStyle::ui` (`UiDeclarations`, `UiStyle`), with `OutlineStyle`, `OutlineColor`, builder setters and their interpolation. (C12-OUTLINE)
- **The scroll-driven animation properties** (Scroll-driven Animations 1 §2–§4): `scroll-timeline` / `view-timeline` and their longhands, `timeline-scope`, `animation-timeline`'s `scroll()` / `view()` / `<dashed-ident>`, `animation-range` and its longhands with range names (`TimelineAxis`, `TimelineInset`, `RangeBoundary`, …). (C12-SCROLL-DRIVEN)
- **Composite addition** (Web Animations 1 §5.4.4): `Longhand::add` adds a number, length or color onto an underlying value (`false` for a discrete one, which replaces); `Longhand::declared_in` (a block names it) and `affects_layout`. (C12-KEYFRAMES)
- **`@keyframes` and the `animation-*` longhands** (CSS Animations 1 §3–§4, 2 §3): `KeyframesRule` / `Keyframe` / `KeyframeSelector` (`Stylesheet::keyframes`, `KeyframesRule::resolve` cascading the blocks of one offset), the ten longhands and `animation` as computed lists (`AnimationName`, `AnimationDuration`, `IterationCount`, …). (C12-KEYFRAMES)
- **`@starting-style` rules** (CSS Transitions 2 §3): `Rule::starting_style` and `RuleContext::in_starting_style` mark a rule that applies only to an element's starting style. (C12-STARTING)
- **`transition-behavior` and `overlay`** (CSS Transitions 2 §3.1, Position 4 §3.4): `normal | allow-discrete` (`TransitionBehavior`, longhand and in `transition`), and `overlay: none | auto` (`Overlay`), `auto !important` on `:modal` / `:popover-open` in the UA sheet; `display` and `overlay` keep their non-`none` value through a transition. (C12-BEHAVIOR)
- **`linear()` easing and negative delays** (CSS Easing 2 §2.1, Transitions 1 §2.4): `linear(<stops>)` canonicalized into `LinearStop`s, `TimingFunction::ease_before` (the steps' before flag), a negative `transition-delay`; `none` is valid only alone, and the `transition` shorthand serializes its lists repeated cyclically. (C12-TIMING)
- **`interpolate-size` and `calc-size()`** (CSS Values 5 §10–§11): `width` / `height` take `calc-size(<basis>, <sum>)` (`Size::CalcSize`, a sum linear in `size`), and under `interpolate-size: allow-keywords` (inherited) `auto` and the intrinsic keywords interpolate with lengths. (C12-ANIMATABLE)
- **`animation`**: each longhand's animation type from its spec (`AnimationType`, `Longhand`, `animation_type`), the longhands a `transition-property` name covers (`transition_longhands`, flow-relative names by direction), and how each computed value interpolates — whole cells, `calc()` mixes, sRGB colors, discrete steps. (C12-ANIMATABLE, ACID-FIX-14)
- **User-action pseudo-classes after a pseudo-element** (Selectors 4 §3.6.3): `::before:hover`, `::after:active`, `li::marker:hover`, `p::first-letter:hover`, and the legacy `p:before:hover`, parse (each trailing pseudo-class through rdom-core's parser) into `Rule::pseudo_state`, a `UserActionState` set counted in the rule's specificity as pseudo-classes; `:focus`, `:focus-visible` and `:focus-within` parse and never match a pseudo-element. A pseudo-class after another pseudo-element, or a logical combination there, drops the rule. (C10-PSEUDO-CHAINS)
- **`::details-content`** (HTML §15.5.20): `PseudoElementTarget::DetailsContent`; the UA sheet's `details::details-content { display: block }` replaces `details:not([open]) > *:not(summary) { display: none }`. (C10-DETAILS-CONTENT)
- **`::before::marker` / `::after::marker`** (CSS Pseudo-Elements 4 §4, CSS Lists 3 §3.1): `PseudoElementTarget::BeforeMarker` / `AfterMarker`, the one nesting of pseudo-elements CSS defines — two pseudo-elements in the specificity, cut to the `::marker` properties, taking trailing `:hover` / `:active`; any other nesting is still an invalid selector. The UA's `::marker { white-space: pre }` covers them. (C10G-PSEUDO-MARKER)
- **`::highlight(<name>)`** (CSS Custom Highlight API 1 §5.1): the selector suffix parses (`PseudoElementTarget::Highlight(name)`, the name case-sensitive, the pseudo-element name ASCII case-insensitive); its rules — and `::selection`'s — keep only `color`, `background-color`, the text decoration properties and custom properties (`TuiStyle::highlight_subset`, CSS Pseudo-Elements 4 §3.2). (C10-HIGHLIGHT)
- **`::first-line` and `::first-letter` rules keep their properties** (CSS Pseudo-Elements 4 §2.2.1, §2.3.1): `TuiStyle::first_line_subset` — the font, color, `opacity`, background and text decoration properties, `text-transform`, `letter-spacing`, `word-spacing` — cuts a `::first-line` rule (and, as before, a `::placeholder` one, which now keeps `text-transform` and the spacings and every background property too); the new `TuiStyle::first_letter_subset` adds `line-height`, `vertical-align`, `float` and the margins, padding and borders (flow-relative ones mapped by direction) for a `::first-letter` rule. (C10-FIRST)
- **The list properties and `::marker`** (CSS Lists 3 §3): `list-style-type` (`ListStyleType`: a counter style, a string, `none`), `list-style-position` (`ListStylePosition`), `list-style-image` (`ListStyleImage`, kept and inert), the `list-style` shorthand, `marker-side` (`MarkerSide`) — inherited, with builders, parsers and serializers; `PseudoElementTarget::Marker`, whose rules keep only the properties that apply to a marker (`TuiStyle::marker_subset`). The UA sheet gives `ul` / `menu` `disc`, `ol` `decimal`, nested bullet lists `circle` then `square` (HTML §15.3.8) and `::marker { white-space: pre }`. (C10-LIST-ITEM)
- **Author counter styles** (CSS Counter Styles 3 §3, §5): `CounterStyle::Symbols` (`symbols()`, every `<symbols-type>`); `parse_counter_style` (a `<counter-style>` from tokens), `apply_descriptor` / `check_rule` (the `@counter-style` grammar); `Stylesheet::counter_styles` / `define_counter_style` (`CounterStyleDefinition`: name, rule, layer; `Stylesheet::append` keeps them); `CounterStyleRegistry` (author definitions over the predefined styles, a `CounterStyleLookup`); `is_counter_style_name`. (C10-COUNTER-STYLE)
- **`counter-set` and `counter-reset: reversed()`** (CSS Lists 3 §4.2–§4.3): `TuiStyle::counter_set` / `ComputedStyle::counter_set`, the builder, the parser and serializer; `CounterOp::reversed`, `is_auto_reversed`. The UA sheet makes `li` `display: list-item` and resets `ol[reversed]` reversed (HTML §15.3.8). (C10-COUNTERS)
- **Every simple predefined counter style** (CSS Counter Styles 3 §6), table-driven: each is a `CounterStyleRule` (the descriptors of `@counter-style`: `System`, symbols, additive symbols, `negative`, `prefix`, `suffix`, `CounterRange`, `pad`, `fallback`, `SpeakAs`) — the numeric styles of every script (`decimal-leading-zero`, `arabic-indic`, `devanagari`, `cjk-decimal`, `thai`, …), the alphabetic ones (`lower-greek`, `hiragana`, `katakana`, the `-iroha` orders), the additive ones (`lower-roman` / `upper-roman`, `hebrew`, `armenian`, `georgian`), `disc` / `circle` / `square` / `disclosure-open` / `disclosure-closed` and the fixed `cjk-earthly-branch` / `cjk-heavenly-stem`. One generator (§3.1) handles range, fallback, `negative` and `pad`; `CounterStyle::format` / `format_in` / `format_with` (any `CounterStyleLookup`) and `marker_text` (with prefix and suffix); `predefined(name)`, `predefined_names()`, `Predefined`. A representation is capped at `MAX_REPRESENTATION_CHARS` (60 code points, §3.1) and a fallback / `extends` chain at `MAX_FALLBACK_DEPTH` (8); `ContentContext::format_counter`. (C10-COUNTERS)
- **`quotes`** (CSS Generated Content 3 §2.1): `Quotes` (`Auto`, `None`, `MatchParent`, `Pairs` of `QuotePair`; `pair(level, lang)`, `explicit(lang)`), `auto_quotes(lang)` (the marks of a content language), `TuiStyle::quotes` / `ComputedStyle::quotes` (inherited), the builder, `parse_quotes`, the serializer. (C10-QUOTES)
- **The full `content` grammar** (CSS Generated Content 3 §2, CSS Lists 3 §4.3): `counters(name, "sep"[, style])`, the four `<quote>` keywords and alt text after `/` (strings, counters, `attr()`; kept on `ComputedStyle::content_alt`, never painted) parse, resolve and serialize; `QuoteKind`; `Content::resolve_alt`, `uses_quotes`, `quotes`. The UA sheet quotes `<q>` (`q::before { content: open-quote }`, `q::after { content: close-quote }`, HTML §15.3.6). (C10-CONTENT)
- **The CSS 2.1 single-colon pseudo-elements** (Selectors 4 §15): `:before`, `:after`, `:first-line` and `:first-letter` are their pseudo-elements, with a pseudo-element's specificity; pseudo-element names match in any ASCII case (`::BEFORE`). `PseudoElementTarget::FirstLine` / `FirstLetter`: `::first-line` / `::first-letter` rules parse and are stored, and style nothing until C10-FIRST. (C10-LEGACY-COLON)
- **`ComputedStyle::is_atomic_inline`** (CSS Display 3 §2.4): `inline-block`, `inline flow-root`, `inline-flex`, `inline-grid` — the predicate the cascade (decoration propagation) and layout share, moved out of rdom-tui's render code. (C9G-MISC-CORRECTNESS)
- **`TextAlignKeyword`** (CSS Text 3 §6.1): the `text-align` shorthand's value — a `TextAlign` or `justify-all` — with `longhands()`; `TuiStyle::text_align` takes it, so `.text_align(TextAlignKeyword::JustifyAll)` justifies every line. **`FontStretchKeyword`**: `font-stretch`'s width keywords, typed. **`ImportantMask::WHITE_SPACE`** is kept, the shorthand's two longhands. (C9G-TYPES)
- **`letter-spacing` and `word-spacing`** (CSS Text 3 §9.1, §9.2): `Spacing` (`Normal`, `Cells`, `Calc`; `cells()` the used whole cells, floored, never negative), `TextStyle` / `TextDeclarations::letter_spacing` / `word_spacing`, the builders, `ImportantMask::LETTER_SPACING` / `WORD_SPACING`; inherited. A pixel or font-relative length is invalid. (C9G-LETTER-SPACING)
- **The font properties** (CSS Fonts 4): `font-weight` (`FontWeight`: `normal`, `bold`, `bolder`, `lighter`, `<number [1,1000]>`; `value(parent)` by §2.2's relative-weight table, `is_bold`), `font-style` (`FontStyle` with `oblique <angle>`; `is_italic`), the inert `font-size` (`FontSize`, `FontSizeKeyword`), `font-family` (`FontFamily`, `SystemFont`), `font-stretch` / `font-width` (`FontStretch`, its keywords `FontStretchKeyword` with `keyword()` / `percent()`), `font-variant` (`FontVariant`, CSS 2.1's form, `#[non_exhaustive]`), and the `font` shorthand (`FontShorthand`, system font keywords, `/ line-height`), all inherited, with the builders (`font_weight`, …) and the parsers / serializers. (C9-FONT)
- **The text decoration properties** (CSS Text Decoration 3 / 4): `text-decoration-line` (`TextDecorationLine`, with `overline` and `blink`), `text-decoration-style` (`TextDecorationStyle`), `text-decoration-color`, `text-decoration-thickness` (`TextDecorationThickness`), `text-underline-offset` (`TextUnderlineOffset`), `text-underline-position` (`TextUnderlinePosition`), `text-decoration-skip-ink` (`TextDecorationSkipInk`), the full `text-decoration` shorthand (any order, shortest serialization), the builders and parsers; `ComputedStyle::text_decoration` (`TextDecorations`, the color resolved) and `applied_decorations` (`AppliedDecorations`: the lines drawn on the element's text after propagation, §2.1). The three placement properties inherit (`TextStyle`). `Modifier::{OVERLINED, UNDERLINE_DOUBLE, UNDERLINE_CURLY, UNDERLINE_DOTTED, UNDERLINE_DASHED}`. (C9-DECORATION)
- **`vertical-align`** (CSS 2.1 §10.8.1): `VerticalAlign` (the eight keywords, `Rows`, `Calc`; `computed(line_height, cx)` — a percentage of the element's line height — `raise()`, `keyword()`), `TuiStyle::vertical_align` / `ComputedStyle::vertical_align` (not inherited), the builder, `parse_vertical_align` / `serialize_vertical_align`; the UA sheet aligns `sub` / `sup` (HTML §15.3.4). (C9-VERTICAL-ALIGN)
- **`line-height`** (CSS Inline 3 §5.1): `LineHeight` (`Normal`, `Number`, `Rows`, `Calc`; `computed(cx)`, `rows()`, `half_leading()`), `TextStyle::line_height` / `TextDeclarations::line_height` (inherited with the CSS Text group), the builder, `parse_line_height` / `serialize_line_height`. `lh` / `rlh` are context units now (`CalcUnit::needs_context`), made absolute at computed-value time with the viewport units: `UnitContext` (viewport, `lh`, `rlh`), `CalcExpr::absolutize_in`, `ComputedStyle::resolve_context_units` (`resolve_viewport_units` is it with one-row line heights). (C9-LINE-HEIGHT, C2-LH)
- **`text-wrap` (the shorthand of `text-wrap-mode` and `text-wrap-style`) and `text-wrap-style`** (CSS Text 4): `TextWrapStyle` (`auto` / `balance` / `stable` / `pretty` / `avoid-short-last-line`), `TextStyle::text_wrap_style` / `TextDeclarations::text_wrap_style`, the builder, `parse_text_wrap` / `parse_text_wrap_style`. (C9-TEXT-WRAP)
- **`text-align` (the shorthand of `text-align-all` and `text-align-last`), `text-align-last`, `text-justify`** (CSS Text 3 §6): `TextAlign` (`physical(rtl)`), `TextAlignLast` (`align()`, `of()`), `TextJustify` (`distribute` parses as `InterCharacter`), the `TextStyle` / `TextDeclarations` fields, `TuiStyle::text_align` (both longhands), `text_align_all` / `text_align_last` / `text_justify`, `parse_text_align` / `parse_text_align_all` / `parse_text_align_last` / `parse_text_justify`; `match-parent` computes in the cascade. (C9-TEXT-ALIGN)
- **`text-indent`** (CSS Text 3 §8.1): `TextIndent { length, hanging, each_line }` (`cells(n)`, `resolve(width)`), `TextStyle::text_indent` / `TextDeclarations::text_indent`, the builder, `parse_text_indent`; viewport units resolve at computed-value time. (C9-TEXT-INDENT)
- **`text-transform`** (CSS Text 3 / 4 §2.1): `TextTransform { case, full_width, full_size_kana, math_auto }` (`NONE`, `is_none()`), `TextCase`, `TextStyle::text_transform` / `TextDeclarations::text_transform`, the builder, `parse_text_transform` / `serialize_text_transform`. (C9-TEXT-TRANSFORM)
- **`tab-size`** (CSS Text 3 §4.2): `TabSize` (`Number`, `Length`; `cells()`), `TextStyle::tab_size` / `TextDeclarations::tab_size`, the builder, `parse_tab_size`. (C9-TAB-SIZE)
- **`word-break`, `overflow-wrap` (and its legacy name `word-wrap`), `line-break`, `hyphens`** (CSS Text 3 §5.2, §5.5, §5.3, §6.1): `WordBreak`, `OverflowWrap`, `LineBreak`, `Hyphens` (`keyword()`), fields of `TextStyle` / `TextDeclarations`, the builders, `parse_word_break` / `parse_overflow_wrap` / `parse_line_break` / `parse_hyphens`. (C9-BREAKING)
- **`white-space-collapse` and `text-wrap-mode`** (CSS Text 4 §4.1, §6.1), and `white-space: pre-line | break-spaces` with the longhand pair form (`preserve nowrap`): `WhiteSpaceCollapse`, `TextWrapMode`, `WhiteSpace::longhands` / `from_longhands` / `keyword`, `TextStyle` / `TextDeclarations`, the builders, `parse_white_space` / `parse_white_space_collapse` / `parse_text_wrap_mode`; `white-space` serializes as its shortest form. (C9-WHITE-SPACE)
- `TuiStyle::webkit_box` / `ComputedStyle::webkit_box`: whether `display` was the Compat Standard's `-webkit-box` / `-webkit-inline-box` (written by the `display` parser with its other fields; serialized as written), and `parse::values::is_legacy_box`. (C8G-WEBKIT-CLAMP)
- **`scroll-snap-type`, `scroll-snap-align`, `scroll-snap-stop`** (CSS Scroll Snap 1 §5–§6): `ScrollSnapType` (`None`, `Snap(ScrollSnapAxis, ScrollSnapStrictness)`), `ScrollSnapAxis` (`physical()`), `ScrollSnapStrictness`, `ScrollSnapAlign { block, inline }` of `SnapAlign`, `ScrollSnapStop`, the `TuiStyle` / `ComputedStyle` fields and builders, `parse_scroll_snap_type` / `_align` / `_stop`. (C8-SNAP)
- **`scroll-padding` and `scroll-margin`** (CSS Scroll Snap 1 §4): the shorthands, the `-top` / `-right` / `-bottom` / `-left` longhands and the flow-relative longhands and shorthands — `ScrollPadding` (`Auto`, `Length(PaddingValue)`, `resolve`), `TuiStyle::scroll_padding_*` / `scroll_margin_*` with their builders, `ComputedStyle::scroll_padding_*` / `scroll_margin_*` (`i16` cells), `parse_scroll_padding(_shorthand)` / `parse_scroll_margin(_shorthand)`. (C8-SCROLL-PADDING)
- **`overscroll-behavior`** (CSS Overscroll Behavior 1 §3): the shorthand (one or two values), `-x` / `-y`, and `-block` / `-inline` as their `horizontal-tb` aliases — `OverscrollBehavior` (`keyword`, `chains`), `TuiStyle::overscroll_behavior_x` / `_y` with their builders, `ComputedStyle::overscroll_behavior_x` / `_y`, `parse_overscroll_behavior` / `parse_overscroll_behavior_shorthand`. (C8-OVERSCROLL)
- **`scrollbar-width` and `scrollbar-color`** (CSS Scrollbars 1 §2–§3) and `scrollbar-gutter: stable both-edges`: `ScrollbarWidth`, `ScrollbarColor` (`Auto`, `Colors { thumb, track }`, inherited), `NATIVE_SCROLLBAR_TRACK` / `NATIVE_SCROLLBAR_THUMB` (the platform colors the UA's `::scrollbar*` rules use too), `TuiStyle::scrollbar_width` / `scrollbar_color` with their builders, `ComputedStyle::scrollbar_width` / `scrollbar_color`, `parse_scrollbar_width` / `parse_scrollbar_color`. (C8-SCROLLBAR)
- **`float` and `clear`** (CSS 2.1 §9.5.1 / §9.5.2, with CSS Logical 1 §2.3's `inline-start` / `inline-end`): `Float` (`keyword`, `side`), `FloatSide`, `Clear` (`keyword`, `sides`), `TuiStyle::float` / `clear` with their builders, `ComputedStyle::float` / `clear`, `parse_float` / `parse_clear`. (C8-FLOAT)
- **`line-clamp`, `max-lines`, `block-ellipsis`, `continue`** (CSS Overflow 4 §4) and the legacy `-webkit-line-clamp`, `-webkit-box-orient` and `display: -webkit-box` / `-webkit-inline-box` (as `flex` / `inline-flex`): `BlockEllipsis`, `Continue`, `BoxOrient`, `TuiStyle::max_lines` / `block_ellipsis` / `continue_` / `webkit_box_orient`, `ComputedStyle::line_clamp_container`, `parse_line_clamp` and the longhand parsers. (C8-LINE-CLAMP)
- **`text-overflow`** (CSS Overflow 4 §3): `clip` / `ellipsis` / `<string>`, one or two values — `TextOverflow` (`one`, `two`, `values`, `line_sides`, `is_clip`), `TextOverflowSide`, `parse_text_overflow`, `TuiStyle::text_overflow`. (C8-TEXT-OVERFLOW)
- **`overflow: clip`, the two-value `overflow`, `overflow-clip-margin`, `overflow-block` / `overflow-inline`** (CSS Overflow 3 §3.1–§3.2): `Overflow::Clip`, `OverflowClipMargin` (`TuiStyle::overflow_clip_margin`), `parse_overflow_shorthand` / `parse_overflow_clip_margin`, `Overflow::is_scrollable` / `clips` / `keyword`, `ComputedStyle::is_scroll_container` / `clips_overflow` / `normalize_overflow`; `overflow` serializes `<x> <y>` when the axes differ. (C8-OVERFLOW-CLIP)
- `CalcUnit::Px`: the CSS pixel that pixel math functions (`border-width: calc(2px + 1in)`, radii, shadows) evaluate in; `CalcUnit::parse` never gives it, so a cell length cannot hold a pixel. (C5G-PERF-AND-TESTS)
- `TuiStyle::substituted_pending(vars, cx)`: only a block's `var()`-pending declarations, substituted onto an empty style with the block's importance bits — the cascade's per-element path, without copying the block. (C1G-VAR-COST)
- **The value tokenizer decodes identifier escapes** (CSS Syntax 3 §4.3.7) through `rdom_core::css_syntax`, in property names and keyword values (`display: fl\65x`); a selector list no longer splits on an escaped comma. New `Cursor::rest` / `Cursor::advance`. (C1-ESCAPES)
- **`revert`** is accepted for every property (ASCII case-insensitive) and serializes as written. (C1-REVERT)
- **`subgrid`** (CSS Grid 2 §9): `GridTemplate::Subgrid(LineNameList)` — `subgrid <line-name-list>?` on `grid-template-columns` / `-rows`, the names as `LineNameItem::Names` / `Repeat` (`repeat(<n> | auto-fill, …)`), `LineNameList::expand` / `explicit_lines` — parsed and serialized as written. (C7-SUBGRID)
- **The `grid-template` and `grid` shorthands** (CSS Grid 2 §7.4, §7.8): `none`, `<rows> / <columns>`, and the areas form with row sizes and line names (`[top] "a a" 1 / 2 1fr`); `grid` adds `auto-flow [dense]` and resets the implicit grid. Serialized in the browsers' shortest form, or not at all. (C7-GRID-AREAS)
- **The `all` shorthand** (CSS Cascade 4 §3.2): `all: initial | inherit | unset | revert | revert-layer` sets every dispatch-table property but `direction`, `unicode-bidi` and custom properties; derived from `PROPERTY_NAMES`. (C1-ALL)
- **Cascade layers in the data model** (CSS Cascade 5 §6.4): `Rule::layer`, `Stylesheet::layers` / `declare_layer` / `declare_anonymous_layer`, `RuleContext::default().in_layer(…)`, `LayerOrder::new(&sheets)` and `Stylesheet::append`; `revert-layer` for every property. (C1-LAYER)
- **`StyleSelector`**: a rule's selector list parsed once (`StyleSelector::parse` / `parse_nested`, `nesting_list()`), added with `Stylesheet::add_style_rule(&selector, style, ctx)` at a `RuleContext` (layer, scope); `Stylesheet::rule` / `add_rule` go through it. (C1-NESTING)
- **`@scope` in the data model** (CSS Cascade 6 §2.5): `Rule::scope`, `Stylesheet::scopes` / `declare_scope(Scope::new(start, end, parent))`, the sheet's `owner_node` / `set_owner_node` (a prelude-less `@scope`'s root), `StyleSelector::parse_scoped`; `append` carries scopes. (C1-SCOPE)
- **`Import` records** (CSS Cascade 5 §3): `Stylesheet::imports()` (URL, layer, `supports()` / media text) and `record_import`; `append` carries them. (C1-IMPORT)
- **`var()` in every property** (CSS Variables 1 §3): `PendingDeclaration`, the `rdom_style::backend` hooks (`contains_var`, `valid_var_syntax`, `substitute`, `resolve_custom_properties`, `set_parsed`, `set_unset`) and `TuiStyle::substituted(&vars, &cx)`; `tui_style.rs` is now `tui_style/`. (C1-VAR-ANY)
- **Registered custom properties** (CSS Properties and Values API 1): the `registration` module (`PropertySyntax`, `SyntaxComponent`, `Multiplier`, `PropertyRegistration::new`) and `Stylesheet::register_property` / `registered_properties`; a custom property in `transition-property` keeps its case. (C1-PROPERTY)
- A computed-value step for `backend::resolve_custom_properties` (`SubstitutionContext::with_computed`): dependents substitute a property's computed value (CSS Properties and Values API 1 §2.4). (C1G-REGISTERED-ORDER)
- `backend::MAX_SUBSTITUTED_TOKENS` (65 536): the most tokens one `var()` substitution may produce (CSS Variables 1 §3.3). (C1G-VAR-TOKENS)
- `Scope::owner` / `Scope::owner_in(sheet)`: the owner node of an `@scope`'s sheet, recorded by `Stylesheet::append`, so a prelude-less `@scope` keeps its root in a merged sheet (CSS Cascade 6 §2.5.1). (C1G-IMPORT-EDGES)
- **`<percentage>` on every length-bearing property** (CSS Values 4 §5.6): `padding`, `margin`, the insets, `inset` (also `calc()`), `min-*` / `max-*`, and `opacity` (`50%` is `0.5`); a lone percentage serializes as written. New `parse_max_size`. (C2-PERCENT)
- **Fractional flex factors** (CSS Flexbox §7.1): `flex: 1.5 0.5 0%`, `flex-shrink: 1.5`, `1.5fr` parse as `<number [0,∞]>`; `parse_flex_factor`, `numeric::number`. (C2-NUMBER)
- **`min()` / `max()` / `clamp()`** (CSS Values 4 §10.2) wherever `calc()` is accepted, nested freely; `clamp()` takes `none`; NaN is 0 (§10.9). `CalcExpr::Function { func: MathFunction, args }`, `CalcExpr::NoBound`, `MathFunction::{Min, Max, Clamp}`. (C2-MINMAX)
- **`round()` / `mod()` / `rem()` / `abs()` / `sign()`** (CSS Values 4 §10.3, §10.7), with `round`'s `nearest` / `up` / `down` / `to-zero`: `MathFunction::{Round(RoundingStrategy), Mod, Rem, Abs, Sign}`, `RoundingStrategy`. (C2-STEPPED)
- **Trigonometric and exponential functions and constants** (CSS Values 4 §10.4–§10.7: `sin()` … `exp()`, `e`, `pi`, `infinity`, `NaN`), type-checked (`CalcExpr::kind`, `CalcKind::{Number, Length, Angle}`); `<number>` math in `opacity` and the flex factors. (C2-TRIG)
- **`ch`** (CSS Values 4 §6.1.1): one column, in every length and math function. New `CalcExpr::Dimension { value, unit }` and `CalcUnit` (`#[non_exhaustive]`); a registered `<length>` takes unit dimensions. (C2-CH)
- **`lh` / `rlh`** (CSS Values 4 §6.1.1): `CalcUnit::{Lh, Rlh}`, the element's and the root's line height (C9-LINE-HEIGHT). (C2-LH)
- **Viewport-percentage units** (CSS Values 4 §6.1.2): `vw` … `vmax`, `sv*` / `lv*` / `dv*`, against the terminal. `CalcUnit::Viewport(ViewportUnit)`, `Viewport`, `ResolveCtx::with_viewport`, `CalcExpr::absolutize` / `needs_context`, `ComputedStyle::resolve_viewport_units`. (C2-VIEWPORT, C2G-VIEWPORT-FIELDS)
- **`<angle>`** (CSS Values 4 §7.1): `deg`, `grad`, `rad`, `turn` (`CalcUnit::{Deg, Grad, Rad, Turn}`) in trigonometric functions; `parse::values::parse_angle`; `@property` `syntax: '<angle>'` (`SyntaxComponent::Angle`). (C2-ANGLE)
- **The full `aspect-ratio` grammar** (CSS Sizing 4 §5.1): `auto`, a lone number, fractional terms, `<number>` math, `auto && <ratio>`; serialized `16 / 9`; a zero term behaves as `auto` (`AspectRatio::value`). (C2-RATIO)
- **`attr()` in any property** (CSS Values 5 §8.7), typed (`type(<syntax>)`, `number`, units, `raw-string`), substituted like `var()` with Values 5's fallback rules: `backend::contains_substitution`, `backend::AttrLookup`, `SubstitutionContext::with_attrs`, `has_substitution`. (C2-ATTR)
- **Math functions in `<integer>` properties and registered numeric syntaxes** (CSS Values 4 §10.9): `z-index: calc(1 + 1)`; `CalcKind::Percent` (`CalcKind` `#[non_exhaustive]`), `CalcExpr::kind_as_number`; `calc::to_cells` and `parse::values::MAX_ANGLE_DEGREES` public. (C2G-CALC-SEMANTICS)
- `Stylesheet::version()`: a process-unique stamp renewed by every mutation (a clone gets its own) — what a cache built from a sheet set compares. (C2G-STATELESS-REGISTRY)
- `PropertySyntax::computed(value, viewport)`: a registered property's computed value (CSS Properties and Values 1 §2.4); `CalcExpr::linear_parts()`, `registration::length_percentage_text`; `interpolation` covers `<length-percentage>`. (C2G-REGISTERED-ABSOLUTE)
- **A failed substitution says why**: `backend::substitute` returns `Result<_, SubstitutionError>` (`Undefined`, `Cycle`, `InvalidAttr`, `TooLong`, `Syntax`); `backend::Lookup`, `lookup_in`; one `SubstitutionContext { attrs, computed }` replaces the `*_with` / `*_on` variants. (C2G-SUBSTITUTION-ERRORS)
- `Size::cells(basis: Option<u16>) -> Option<u16>` and `Length::cells(basis: i32) -> Option<i32>`: a size or inset in cells, `None` for `auto` (and a percentage against an indefinite basis) — the one conversion layout uses. (C2G-CELLS-CONVERSIONS, C3G-API)
- `Size::percent(p)` / `MinSize::percent(p)` / `MaxSize::percent(p)` (`f32`) and `From<u16> for Size`, so `TuiStyle::width(20)` takes a `u16`. (C2G-REEXPORT-CALC, C3G-API)
- **Modern `rgb()` / `rgba()`** (CSS Color 4 §5.1): space-separated channels, `/ alpha`, `none`, fractions and percentages, math functions; the legacy comma syntax too. `color::serialize_alpha`; a translucent color serializes as `rgba(r, g, b, a)`. (C3-RGB)
- **`currentcolor`** (CSS Color 4 §6.4) in every color property: `TuiColor::parse(css)`, `TuiColor::substitute_vars(vars)`, `TuiColor::resolve(vars, cx)`, `depends_on_element()`. (C3-CURRENTCOLOR)
- **`hsl()` / `hsla()` / `hwb()`** (CSS Color 4 §7, §8): hues as numbers or `<angle>`s, `none`, `/ alpha`, math functions, `hsl()`'s legacy comma syntax; converted to sRGB at parse time. (C3-HSL-HWB)
- **`lab()` / `lch()` / `oklab()` / `oklch()` and `color()`** (CSS Color 4 §9, §10) with the predefined spaces, gamut-mapped into sRGB (§13.2) in `color::{convert, gamut}`; `color::interpolate_oklab(from, to, t)` and `color::interpolate_srgb(from, to, t)` (§12). (C3-LAB, ACID-FIX-14)
- **`color-mix()`** (CSS Color 5 §2) in any rectangular or polar space with the hue methods, Oklab by default, §2.2 normalization and Color 4 §12 interpolation; `color::palette::xterm_rgb(n)` gives a palette index's color. (C3-MIX)
- **Relative color syntax** (CSS Color 5 §4): `from <color>` in every color function, its channels bound as numbers (`hsl(from var(--brand) calc(h + 180) s l)`); a `currentcolor` origin computes at computed-value time. (C3-RELATIVE)
- **System colors** (CSS Color 4 §6.2), the deprecated ones mapped; `Canvas` / `ButtonFace` / `CanvasText` / `FieldText` are the terminal's defaults, the rest the UA palette, now in `color::system` (`color::SystemColor`; mapping in DIVERGENCES). (C3-SYSTEM)
- **`color-scheme` and `light-dark()`** (CSS Color Adjust 1 §2, CSS Color 5 §5): `ColorSchemeList::used(preferred)`, `ColorScheme` (dark by default) with `for_background(color)`, `ColorContext::scheme` / `with_scheme`. (C3-SCHEME)
- **The full `background` shorthand and its longhands** (CSS Backgrounds 3 §3.3–§3.10), stored on `TuiStyle` with builders; only the color paints (DIVERGENCES §1). New `layout::{VisualBox, RepeatStyle, BackgroundRepeat, BackgroundAttachment}`, `parse::values::{parse_background, …}`. (C4-BACKGROUND)
- **`border` and `border-<side>` take a width, a style and a color in any order** (CSS Backgrounds 3 §4.4), omitted ones reset; `parse::values::parse_line_width`, `layout::{Sides, BorderWidth, PaintLength}`, `parse_border_side_shorthand`, `BorderShorthand`. rdom's keywords keep working. (C4-BORDER-SHORTHAND)
- **`border-style` / `-color` / `-width` take one to four values** (CSS Backgrounds 3 §4.1–§4.3), and `border-<side>-color` / `-width` are longhands of their own; `layout::Sides::from_values`, `parse::values::parse_sides`. (C4-BORDER-SIDES)
- **`border-radius` and the four corner longhands** (CSS Backgrounds 3 §5): `BorderRadius`, `layout::Corners`, `parse_border_radius` / `parse_corner_radius`, the `border_radius` builder; a rounded corner draws its arc glyph (DIVERGENCES §2). `border: rounded` is `solid` plus `border-radius: 1`. (C4-RADIUS)
